We formally derive conditions under which this integration preserves safety and demonstrate the performance benefits. We present the design, analysis, and evaluation of SS for AV obstacle detection.

</details>


### [75] [ExecuTorch -- A Unified PyTorch Solution to Run AI Models On-Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08195)
*Mergen Nachin,Digant Desai,Sicheng Stephen Jia,Chen Lai,Mengwei Liu,Jacob Szwejbka,Raziel Alvarez,RJ Ascani,Dave Bort,Manuel Candales,Andrew Caples,Yanan Cao,Zhengxu Chen,Soumith Chintala,Gregory Comer,Tanvir Islam,Songhao Jia,Tarun Karuturi,Jack Khuu,Abhinay Kukkadapu,Tugsbayasgalan Manlaibaatar,Andrew Or,Kimish Patel,Siddartha Pothapragada,Lucy Qiu,Supriya Rao,Orion Reblitz-Richardson,Max Ren,Scott Roy,Anthony Shoumikhin,Scott Wolchok,Guang Yang,Angela Yi,Martin Yuan,Hansong Zhang,Jack Zhang,Jerry Zhang,Shunting Zhang,C. Cagatay Bilgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExecuTorch 是一个 PyTorch 原生边缘AI部署框架，支持从微控制器到大型计算集群的异构设备，实现从研究到生产的无缝部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的AI执行需要低延迟和离线运行，但现有部署方式因硬件碎片化而需要模型转换或在PyTorch生态外重建，部署流程割裂。

Method: ExecuTorch 保持PyTorch语义，支持定制化、量化等优化，并提供可插拔的执行后端，允许研究人员在PyTorch内验证部署行为。

Result: 实现了从嵌入式微控制器到复杂SoC及大型计算集群的统一部署，覆盖可穿戴设备、智能手机等多种设备。

Conclusion: ExecuTorch 桥接了研究与生产之间的鸿沟，使得快速实验和跨异构计算环境的无缝模型部署成为可能。

Abstract: Local execution of AI on edge devices is important for low latency and offline operation. However, deploying models on diverse hardware remains fragmented, often requiring model conversion or complete reimplementation outside the PyTorch ecosystem where the model was originally authored. We introduce ExecuTorch, a unified PyTorch-native deployment framework for edge AI. ExecuTorch enables seamless deployment of machine learning models across heterogeneous compute environments. It scales from embedded microcontrollers to complex system-on-chips (SoCs) with dedicated accelerators, powering devices ranging from wearables and smartphones to large compute clusters. ExecuTorch preserves PyTorch semantics while allowing customization, support for optimizations like quantization, and pluggable execution "backends". These features together enable fast experimentation, allowing researchers to validate deployment behavior entirely within PyTorch, bridging the gap between research and production.

</details>


### [76] [ReplaySCM: A Benchmark for Executable Causal Mechanism Induction from Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08197)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ReplaySCM基准，通过可执行因果机制回放（而非公式字符串匹配）评估语言模型从有限干预证据中归纳因果机制的能力，发现前沿LLM在结构信息隐藏时表现大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果基准主要评估局部答案或图结构，缺乏对语言模型从有限干预证据中归纳可执行因果机制能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建1,300个由潜在布尔结构因果模型生成的二元世界项，要求系统输出受限布尔DSL的机制映射，并通过回放行为（而非语法匹配）评分；设计多种结构信息披露设置（有序、块序、隐藏序、隐藏根）和替代SCM任务，以及支持审计阶梯（原始、额外世界、反例审计）。

Result: 前沿LLM能推断部分功能-父结构，但隐藏顺序或根结构时回放表现大幅下降；支持审计阶梯将局部前驱模式覆盖率从0.8949提升至0.9815至1.0，但有序/隐藏序差距在更强证据下仍存在。

Conclusion: ReplaySCM通过评估有限干预证据下的可执行回放泛化能力，补充了现有因果推理基准，不要求唯一识别潜在SCM。

Abstract: Most causal benchmarks for language models score local answers or graph structure. We introduce ReplaySCM, a 1,300 item benchmark for executable causal mechanism induction from finite interventional evidence. Each item contains binary worlds generated by a latent fully observed acyclic Boolean structural causal model (SCM). A system must output a mechanism map in a restricted Boolean DSL; the submission is parsed, checked for legality and acyclicity, and replayed on training and held-out intervention worlds. Scoring uses replay behavior rather than formula strings, so syntactically different mechanisms receive credit when they behave correctly. ReplaySCM varies the structural information disclosed to the model through Ordered, Block-order, Hidden-order, and Hidden-roots settings, and includes Alternative-SCM tasks that supply a valid reference SCM and ask for a semantically distinct alternative that fits the training worlds, together with a separating intervention and witness. Frontier LLMs infer parts of the functional-parent structure, but held-out replay drops sharply when order or root structure is hidden. We also evaluate a matched support-audit ladder: Original, Extra Worlds, and Counterexample Audit (CEx), that raises mean local predecessor-pattern coverage from 0.8949 to 0.9815 to 1.0; under the audited searches, no discovered semantic alternative remains consistent with the training worlds. The Ordered/Hidden-order gap persists under this stronger evidence. ReplaySCM complements answer-level causal reasoning and graph-discovery benchmarks by evaluating executable replay generalization from finite interventional evidence, without claiming unique identification of the latent SCM.

</details>


### [77] [FairHealth: An Open-Source Python Library for Trustworthy Healthcare AI in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08198)
*Farjana Yesmin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairHealth是一个面向医疗AI的Python库，专注于低资源国家（如孟加拉国）的应用，提供联邦学习、公平性审计、可解释性、低资源分诊、公平分配和数据集加载六大模块，填补了现有工具在公平审计、隐私保护、可解释性及全球南方数据集方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI工具包存在四个关键缺口：缺乏针对生物信号和临床表格数据的公平性审计、缺乏与标准ML工作流兼容的隐私保护联邦学习工具、缺乏适用于低带宽临床决策支持的可解释性工具、没有覆盖全球南方医疗数据集的工具包。FairHealth旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 基于五项同行评审研究成果构建，提供六大模块：fairhealth.federated（带同态加密的联邦学习）、fairhealth.fairness（交叉性公平性指标）、fairhealth.explain（混合模糊-SHAP可解释性）、fairhealth.lowresource（多语言登革热分诊）、fairhealth.equity（公平灾害援助分配）、fairhealth.datasets（公共数据集加载器）。所有数据集公开可用，无需机构数据使用协议。

Result: FairHealth已发布在PyPI上（pip install fairhealth），源代码托管在GitHub上，可直接安装使用。

Conclusion: FairHealth提供了一个统一的模块化框架，弥补了现有工具在公平性、隐私保护、可解释性及全球南方数据集方面的不足，为低资源环境下的可信医疗AI提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present FairHealth, an open-source Python library that provides a unified, modular framework for trustworthy machine learning in healthcare applications, with particular focus on low-resource and low-income country (LMIC) settings such as Bangladesh. FairHealth addresses four critical gaps in existing healthcare AI toolkits: (1) the absence of integrated fairness auditing for biosignals and clinical tabular data; (2) the lack of privacy-preserving federated learning tools compatible with standard ML workflows; (3) missing explainability tools tailored for low-bandwidth clinical decision support; and (4) no existing toolkit covering Global South healthcare datasets. Built from five peer-reviewed research contributions, FairHealth provides six modules covering federated learning with homomorphic encryption (fairhealth.federated), intersectional fairness metrics (fairhealth.fairness), hybrid fuzzy-SHAP explainability (fairhealth.explain), multilingual dengue triage (fairhealth.lowresource), equitable disaster aid allocation (fairhealth.equity), and public dataset loaders (fairhealth.datasets). All datasets used are publicly available without institutional data use agreements. FairHealth is installable via pip install fairhealth(PyPI: pypi.org/project/fairhealth/) and available at https://github.com/Farjana-Yesmin/fairhealth.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Penalization: Diffusion-based Out-of-Distribution Detection and Selective Regularization in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08202)
*Qingjun Wang,Hongtu Zhou,Hang Yu,Junqiao Zhao,Yanping Zhao,Chen Ye,Ziqiao Wang,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DOSER框架，利用扩散模型识别OOD动作，通过选择性正则化区分有益和有害的离群动作，实现更稳定的离线强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL方法对OOD动作采取统一惩罚，未能准确区分不同类型的OOD动作，可能抑制有益的探索。

Method: 训练两个扩散模型分别捕捉行为策略和状态分布，利用单步去噪重建误差作为OOD指标；通过评估预测转移来区分有益/有害OOD动作，选择性抑制风险动作并鼓励高潜力探索。

Result: 理论上证明DOSER是γ-压缩映射且存在唯一不动点，给出渐近性能保证；在多个离线RL基准上，特别是在次优数据集上，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DOSER通过细粒度的OOD区分和选择性正则化，有效解决了离线RL中的过度估计问题，实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) faces a critical challenge of overestimating the value of out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. Existing methods mitigate this issue by penalizing unseen samples, yet they fail to accurately identify OOD actions and may suppress beneficial exploration beyond the behavioral support. Although several methods have been proposed to differentiate OOD samples with distinct properties, they typically rely on restrictive assumptions about the data distribution and remain limited in discrimination ability. To address this problem, we propose DOSER (Diffusion-based OOD Detection and Selective Regularization), a novel framework that goes beyond uniform penalization. DOSER trains two diffusion models to capture the behavior policy and state distribution, using single-step denoising reconstruction error as a reliable OOD indicator. During policy optimization, it further distinguishes between beneficial and detrimental OOD actions by evaluating predicted transitions, selectively suppressing risky actions while encouraging exploration of high-potential ones. Theoretically, we prove that DOSER is a $γ$-contraction and therefore admits a unique fixed point with bounded value estimates. We further provide an asymptotic performance guarantee relative to the optimal policy under model approximation and OOD detection errors. Across extensive offline RL benchmarks, DOSER consistently attains superior performance to prior methods, especially on suboptimal datasets.

</details>


### [79] [LLMs with in-context learning for Algorithmic Theoretical Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08212)
*Anamaria Hell,Leander Thiele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了将大型语言模型（Claude）与计算机代数系统（Maple）结合，用于理论物理中算法计算任务的可行性，并以修改引力理论中的宇宙学扰动为例进行了测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论物理中算法计算日益增多，虽然概念上简单但耗时且容易出错，因此希望利用改进后的大语言模型结合计算机代数系统来自动化完成这些任务。

Method: 将Claude（大语言模型）与Maple（计算机代数系统）接口化，提供充分的上文背景信息，让LLM调用CAS运行时来执行算法任务，并在修改引力理论的宇宙学扰动问题上进行测试。

Result: 前沿的LLM在提供工作示例的情况下能够解决大多数测试问题，同时也发现了典型的失败模式。

Conclusion: 该方法具有可行性，当前已能解决大部分问题，但仍存在失败情况需要改进。

Abstract: There is an increasing number of algorithmic computations in theoretical physics. These, while conceptually simple, can nevertheless be time-consuming and contain subtleties that should not be overlooked. Given the recent improvement of Large Language Models (LLM), it is natural to investigate whether LLMs equipped with a computer algebra system (CAS) runtime and sufficiently informative context can reliably carry out these algorithmic tasks. In this work, we interface Claude with Maple, and apply this framework to cosmological perturbations in modified theories of gravity. We demonstrate the current capabilities of this approach, the typical failures, and how the same can be improved. We find that a frontier LLM supplied with worked examples is able to solve most test problems.

</details>


### [80] [Retrieval Mechanisms Surpass Long-Context Scaling in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08217)
*Rishi Ahuja,Kumar Prateek,Simranjit Singh,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究反驳了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）中“更长上下文提升预测质量”的假设，实验发现随着上下文增长，预测误差反而上升（逆缩放定律），而基于检索增强的RAFT方法以更低的计算成本取得了更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证TSFMs中“输入更多历史数据能提升预测质量”这一从NLP借用的前提是否成立，因为随机领域中远距离历史往往只是高频噪声而非信号。

Method: 在ETTh1基准上测试连续上下文架构（含PatchTST），并与检索增强预测（RAFT）方法对比；RAFT使用固定720步窗口+选择性检索，仅注入最相关的历史片段作为动态外生变量。

Result: 1）长上下文导致逆缩放定律：3000步窗口使性能下降超68%；2）RAFT以MSE 0.379超越长上下文配置和零样本基础模型（Chronos, Moirai），且计算量更少。

Conclusion: 基础模型应转向选择性检索架构，因为注意力机制难以有效忽略历史波动噪声，检索增强能提供模型自身无法从原始序列中构建的上下文归纳偏置。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have borrowed the long context paradigm from natural language processing under the premise that feeding more history into the model improves forecast quality. But in stochastic domains, distant history is often just high-frequency noise, not signal. Hence, the proposed work tests whether this premise actually holds by running continuous context architectures (PatchTST included) through the ETTh1 benchmark. The obtained results contradict the premise: an inverse scaling law shows up clearly, with forecasting error rising as context gets longer. A 3,000-step window causes performance to drop by over 68%, evidence that attention mechanisms are poor at ignoring irrelevant historical volatility. Retrieval-Augmented Forecasting (RAFT) is evaluated as an alternative. RAFT achieves a mean squared error (MSE) of 0.379 with a fixed 720-step window and selective retrieval, outperforming both long-context configurations and zero-shot foundation models (Chronos, Moirai) despite requiring far less computation. In addition, the retrieval step injects only the most relevant historical segments as dynamic exogenous variables, which gives the model a context-informed inductive bias it cannot build on its own from raw sequences. Therefore, foundation models going forward need to shift architecturally toward selective retrieval.

</details>


### [81] [Deep Dreams Are Made of This: Visualizing Monosemantic Features in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08218)
*Adam Szokalski,Mateusz Modrzejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LVO方法，通过稀疏自编码器解开潜在扩散模型中的多义性表征，生成更清晰的视觉概念可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征可视化技术主要用于CNN，难以直接应用于潜在扩散模型；同时模型中的多义性表征阻碍了可解释性分析。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器解耦多义性层表征；潜在空间优化；时间步活动分析；调度匹配噪声注入；特征引导的先验初始化；合适的正则化策略。

Result: 在Style50数据集上微调的Stable Diffusion 1.5上，SAE特征生成的对角线构图、人体、玫瑰、电缆、瀑布泡沫等概念可视化更清晰且与数据集示例相关，基线方法结果则不够连贯。

Conclusion: LVO通过直接揭示激活特征的内容而非下游效应，提供了与数据集示例和特征引导互补的洞察；像素空间的正则化技术可迁移到潜在域但需不同配置。

Abstract: This paper proposes latent visualization by optimization (LVO), a mechanistic interpretability technique that extends feature visualization by optimization - originally developed for convolutional neural networks - to latent diffusion models. LVO employs sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to disentangle polysemantic layer representations into monosemantic features. Key contributions include latent-space optimization, time-step activity analysis, schedule-matched noise injection, prior initialization through feature steering, and suitable regularization strategies. We demonstrate the method on Stable Diffusion 1.5 fine-tuned on the Style50 dataset, showing that SAE features produce clear visualizations of recognizable concepts - including diagonal compositions, human figures, roses, cables, and waterfall foam - that correlate with dataset examples, while the baseline without disentanglement produces less coherent results. We further show that regularization techniques from pixel-space feature visualization transfer to the latent domain, though they require different configurations for the raw-layer and SAE variants. Compared to dataset examples and steering, LVO provides complementary insights by directly revealing what activates a feature rather than its downstream effects.

</details>


### [82] [NoisyCoconut: Counterfactual Consensus via Latent Space Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08221)
*Michael Jerge,David Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NoisyCoconut是一种推理时增强LLM可靠性的方法，通过在潜在轨迹中注入可控噪声生成多样化的推理路径，利用路径间的一致性作为置信度信号，使模型在不确定时能够弃权，无需重新训练或修改参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要大量重新训练来提升LLM可靠性，推理时模型在面对不确定性问题时缺乏有效的置信度评估和弃权机制。

Method: 在推理过程中向模型的潜在表示轨迹注入可控噪声，生成多条不同的推理路径，通过评估这些路径之间的一致性（尤其是完全一致性）来判断模型置信度，从而实现选择性弃权。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，噪声扰动路径的一致性能将错误率从40-70%降至15%以下，通过选择性弃权在数学推理任务上达到超过95%的准确率。

Conclusion: NoisyCoconut提供了一种无需重新训练或访问训练数据的实用路径，有效提升了LLM输出的可靠性，兼容现有模型。

Abstract: This paper presents NoisyCoconut, a novel inference-time method that enhances large language model (LLM) reliability by manipulating internal representations. Unlike fine-tuning methods that require extensive retraining, NoisyCoconut operates directly on model representations during inference and requires no retraining. Rather than training models to reason in latent space, we inject controlled noise into latent trajectories to generate diverse reasoning paths. Agreement among these paths provides a confidence signal, enabling models to abstain when uncertain. We demonstrate that this approach achieves effective coverage-accuracy tradeoffs across multiple reasoning benchmarks without requiring access to training data or modification of model parameters. This approach provides a practical pathway to improving the reliability of LLM outputs while maintaining compatibility with existing models. Our experiments show that unanimous agreement among noise-perturbed paths reduces error rates from 40-70% to below 15%, enabling models to exceed 95% accuracy on mathematical reasoning tasks through selective abstention.

</details>


### [83] [A Simulated Federated Analysis of MS-Induced Brain Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08223)
*Evelyn Trautmann,Joël Federer-Gsponer,Markus C. Elze,José-Tomás Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模拟真实世界联邦研究项目的仿真框架，用于多发性硬化症（MS）患者数据的图像分割和临床数据分析，涵盖联邦生存分析和主成分分析，旨在为联邦学习方法提供测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习等联邦技术在保护隐私的前提下进行多中心临床研究具有潜力，但缺乏能够真实反映临床数据复杂性和异质性的仿真测试平台，尤其是针对多发性硬化症研究场景。

Method: 构建包含图像分割和临床数据分析两个任务的联邦仿真框架，采用联邦生存分析和联邦PCA；使用高保真合成队列模拟MS临床和人口学特征，图像部分使用公开真实数据集；模拟分布式数据治理、站点特定预处理、隔离节点训练及安全聚合等真实联邦工作流。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够模拟真实联邦研究项目工作流程的仿真框架，为MS研究中的联邦学习方法开发、评估和基准测试提供了现实测试环境。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架为多中心MS研究中的联邦学习方法提供了可靠的测试平台，能够帮助研究者开发更有效的隐私保护分析工具。

Abstract: Federated techniques such as federated learning and federated analysis have emerged as a powerful paradigm for enabling multi-center research on sensitive clinical data while preserving patient privacy. In this study, we introduce a simulation framework that emulates a real-world federated research project focused on the analysis of multiple sclerosis (MS) patient data. The project comprises two components: an image segmentation task and a clinical data analysis task, where federated variants of survival analysis and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) are employed. To capture the complexity and heterogeneity of real clinical datasets, we construct a federation of high-fidelity synthetic cohorts designed to mirror MS-related clinical and demographic characteristics, while the imaging component leverages publicly available real-world datasets. Our simulation replicates key elements of authentic federated workflows, including distributed data governance, site-specific preprocessing, model training across isolated nodes, and the secure aggregation of analytical outputs. This framework provides a realistic testbed for developing, evaluating, and benchmarking federated learning methods in the context of MS research.

</details>


### [84] [TRAM: Training Approximate Multiplier Structures for Low-Power AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08231)
*Chang Meng,Hanyu Wang,Yuyang Ye,Mingfei Yu,Wayne Burleson,Giovanni De Micheli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAM方法，通过联合优化近似乘法器结构和AI模型参数，在保持较小精度损失的同时显著降低AI加速器功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器功耗日益重要，近似计算可在保持精度的同时降低功耗，而乘法器是AI模型中功耗最高的组件之一，现有方法将乘法器设计与模型训练分离，未进行联合优化。

Method: 提出TRAM方法，联合优化近似乘法器(AxM)结构和AI模型参数，实现端到端的低功耗近似计算。

Result: 在CIFAR-10的CNN上实现最高25.05%的乘法器功耗降低，在ImageNet的视觉Transformer上实现最高27.09%的功耗降低。

Conclusion: TRAM通过联合优化设计，相比现有最先进近似乘法器在多种AI模型上取得了显著的功耗降低效果。

Abstract: Reducing power consumption in AI accelerators is increasingly important. Approximate computing can reduce power consumption while keeping the accuracy loss small. Since multipliers are power-hungry components in AI models, this paper focuses on synthesizing low-power approximate multipliers (AxMs). Unlike prior works that design AxMs separately from AI model training, we present TRAM, which jointly optimizes the AxM structure and AI model parameters to lower power with small accuracy loss. Experiments show that compared to state-of-the-art AxMs, TRAM achieves up to 25.05% AxM power reduction on CNNs with CIFAR-10, and reduces power by up to 27.09% on vision transformers with ImageNet.

</details>


### [85] [When Does Value-Aware KV Eviction Help? A Fixed-Contract Diagnostic for Non-Monotone Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08234)
*Ruijie Zhang,Haozhe Liang,Da Chang,Li Hu,Fanqi Kong,Huaxiao Yin,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存压缩的选择器失败原因进行了诊断分析，提出了固定契约诊断方法，并通过实验评估了支持恢复、输出价值排序和耦合证据保留的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于KV缓存的内存和带宽开销，KV压缩通过仅保留部分缓存来降低成本，但仅凭任务准确性无法判断选择器成功或失败的原因。

Method: 提出一种固定契约诊断方法，固定选择器设置，每次改变一个决策槽。对于价值排序，探针结合块的注意力质量与移除该块后估计的输出变化。

Result: 在LongBench上针对三个模型和两个预算，探针在72.6%的正边际单元和32.4%的非正边际单元上呈阳性。NeedleBench M-RT在32k和RULER 8k检查探针支持分支检索下的闭包，264单元符号评估将支持恢复和输出价值排序与边界附近的杠杆效应分开。

Conclusion: 得出的顺序是：先恢复解码侧证据，再对其输出价值排序，最后在投影过程中保留耦合证据。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by the memory and bandwidth cost of reading large KV caches during decoding. KV compression reduces this cost by keeping only part of the cache, but task accuracy alone does not identify why a selector succeeds or fails. A selector can fail at three steps: it may miss the evidence future decoding needs, give high scores to tokens that do not affect the output, or break related evidence when fitting scores into a small cache. We introduce a fixed-contract diagnostic that holds the selector's setup fixed and changes one decision slot at a time. For value ranking, the probe combines a block's attention mass with the estimated output change from removing it. On LongBench across three models and two budgets, the probe is positive on 72.6% of positive-margin cells and 32.4% of nonpositive-margin cells. NeedleBench M-RT at 32k and a RULER 8k check probe support closure under branched retrieval, and a 264-cell sign evaluation separates support recovery and output-value ranking from leverage effects near the boundary. The resulting order is to recover decode-side evidence, rank its output value, and preserve coupled evidence during projection.

</details>


### [86] [Distributional Spectral Diagnostics for Localizing Grokking Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08237)
*Ziyue Wang,Yufeng Ying,Takafumi Kanamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究grokking现象的过渡定位问题，提出基于Hankel动态模式分解（DMD）的诊断方法，通过重建残差、频谱和有效秩来预测模型泛化能力的转变。在模块加法Transformer实验中，该方法在运行级别达到约0.93的AUROC，并能提前发出预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在grokking现象中，模型先拟合训练数据而测试准确率低，之后才泛化。本文想知道能否在测试准确率上升之前，从观测到的训练轨迹中定位这一转变。

Method: 将任务相关的可观测变量总结为经验分布，映射到Wasserstein/分位数坐标，再通过Hankel动态模式分解（DMD）进行分析，利用重建残差、频谱和有效秩作为诊断输出。

Result: 在保留的模块加法Transformer运行中，残差的AUROC≈0.93，能有效区分grokking与非grokking；在高残差窗口中，扰动偏差约为低残差窗口的3倍；残差排序与扰动敏感性对齐，而非参数范数排序。

Conclusion: 该残差可作为窗口级别的监测和定位信号，但并非通用的早期预警预测器或干预规则。

Abstract: In grokking, a model first fits the training data while test accuracy remains low, and only later begins to generalize. We ask whether this transition can be localized from observed training trajectories before the test accuracy rises, and formulate grokking transition localization as a diagnostic problem with an explicit threshold/FPR/lead-time trade-off. Task-dependent observables are summarized as empirical distributions, mapped to Wasserstein/quantile coordinates, and analyzed by Hankel dynamic mode decomposition (DMD); the resulting reconstruction residual, together with spectrum and effective rank, forms the diagnostic output. On held-out modular-addition Transformer runs, the residual achieves AUROC \(\approx \) 0.93 for grokking-vs-non-grokking discrimination at the run level; under a fixed sustained-threshold operating rule, true-positive alarms can precede onset, with lead time reported jointly with false-alarm rate and uncertainty intervals. Perturbation experiments show that, in the tested \(wd=1\) pool, high-residual windows exhibit about \(3\times\) larger short-horizon perturbation deviation than low-residual windows. In a same-data norm-window control, perturbation sensitivity aligns with the residual ordering rather than total-parameter-norm ordering, suggesting that the residual is not merely a total-norm proxy at the window level in the studied \(wd=1\) dynamics. Norm signals remain strong run-level regime indicators, and log-probability performs best among the observables tested under the current protocol. We position the residual as a window-level monitoring and localization signal in the studied modular-arithmetic Transformer settings, not a universal early-warning predictor or an intervention rule.

</details>


### [87] [Path-Coupled Bellman Flows for Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08253)
*Boyang Xu,Qing Zou,Siqin Yang,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出路径耦合贝尔曼流（PCBF），一种基于流匹配的连续时间分布强化学习方法，通过源一致贝尔曼耦合路径学习回报分布，解决了边界不匹配和高方差自举问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布强化学习方法中，有限支撑或基于分位数的方法依赖投影，而基于流的方法存在流源的边界不匹配问题，或当当前噪声与后继噪声独立时引起高方差自举问题。

Method: PCBF采用源一致贝尔曼耦合路径：当前路径从t=0的基先验出发，在t=1达到贝尔曼目标，并在中间时刻与后继流保持逐点仿射关系（不要求所有t的边际分布满足分布贝尔曼不动点）。通过共享基噪声耦合当前流与后继流，并使用λ参数化控制变量目标：λ=0恢复无偏样本贝尔曼目标，λ>0以可控偏差换取方差降低。

Result: 在解析可计算的MRP、OGBench和D4RL上的实验表明，PCBF在分布保真度和训练稳定性方面均有提升，离线RL性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PCBF通过路径耦合与控制变量技术有效解决了边界不匹配和高方差问题，为连续时间分布强化学习提供了更稳定有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution, but existing finite-support or quantile-based methods rely on projections, while recent flow-based approaches can suffer from \emph{boundary mismatch} at the flow source or from \emph{high-variance} bootstrapping when current and successor noises are independent. We propose Path-Coupled Bellman Flows (PCBF), a continuous-time DRL method that learns return distributions with flow matching using \textbf{source-consistent Bellman-coupled paths}: the current path starts from the required base prior at $t{=}0$, reaches the Bellman target at $t{=}1$, and maintains a pathwise affine relation to the successor flow at intermediate times (without requiring time-$t$ marginals to satisfy a distributional Bellman fixed point for all $t$). PCBF couples current and successor return flows through shared base noise and uses a $λ$-parameterized control-variate target: $λ{=}0$ recovers an unbiased sample Bellman target, while $λ{>}0$ trades controlled bias for variance reduction. Experiments on analytically tractable MRPs, OGBench, and D4RL show improved distributional fidelity and training stability, and competitive offline RL performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Where Reliability Lives in Vision-Language Models: A Mechanistic Study of Attention, Hidden States, and Causal Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08200)
*Logan Mann,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Shikhar Shiromani,Saadullah Ismail,Yi Xia,Emily Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在3-7B视觉语言模型中，注意力图的锐利度几乎无法预测模型回答的正确性（相关系数接近0），而隐藏状态几何、层间边际形成和稀疏的后期层电路才是更可靠的可靠性指标。同时揭示了不同融合架构在可靠性分布上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证普遍直觉——即注意力图越锐利、越集中在查询区域，视觉语言模型的回答就越可信（Attention-Confidence Assumption）是否成立。

Method: 构建VLM可靠性探测（VRP）统一机制流水线，在LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL三个模型家族上比较注意力结构、生成动态和隐藏状态几何与正确性标签的关系，并进行因果神经元消融实验。

Result: 注意力结构与正确性几乎无相关（R_pb≈0.001）；后期隐藏状态线性探测可达AUROC>0.95；自一致性（K=10）是最强行为预测指标（R_pb=0.43）；晚期融合架构可靠性集中于脆弱晚期瓶颈，早期融合架构分布广泛。

Conclusion: 在3-7B VLMs中，可靠性更可靠地来源于隐藏状态几何、层间边际形成和稀疏后期层电路，而非注意力图锐利度。不同融合架构对可靠性监控器的设计有直接影响。

Abstract: A pervasive intuition holds that vision-language models (VLMs) are most trustworthy when their attention maps look sharp: concentrated attention on the queried region should imply a confident, calibrated answer. We test this Attention-Confidence Assumption directly. We instrument three open-weight VLM families (LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL; 3-7B parameters) with a unified mechanistic pipeline -- the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP) -- that compares attention structure, generation dynamics, and hidden-state geometry against a single correctness label. Three results emerge. (i) Attention structure is a near-zero predictor of correctness (R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95% CI [-0.034,0.036]; R_pb(H_s,y)=-0.012, [-0.047,0.024] on a pooled n=3,090 split), even though attention remains causally necessary for feature extraction (top-30% patch masking drops accuracy by 8.2-11.3 pp, p<0.001). (ii) Reliability becomes legible later in the computation: a single hidden-state linear probe reaches AUROC>0.95 on POPE for two of three families, and self-consistency at K=10 is the strongest behavioral predictor we measure at 10x inference cost (R_pb=0.43). (iii) Causal neuron-level ablations expose a sharp architectural split with direct monitor-design implications: late-fusion LLaVA concentrates reliability in a fragile late bottleneck (-8.3 pp object-identification accuracy after top-5 probe-neuron ablation), whereas early-fusion PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute it widely and absorb destruction of ~50% of their peak-layer hidden dimension with <=1 pp degradation. The takeaway is narrow but consequential: in 3-7B VLMs, reliability is read more reliably off hidden-state geometry, layer-wise margin formation, and sparse late-layer circuits than off attention-map sharpness.

</details>


### [89] [Auto-Rubric as Reward: From Implicit Preferences to Explicit Multimodal Generative Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08354)
*Juanxi Tian,Fengyuan Liu,Jiaming Han,Yilei Jiang,Yongliang Wu,Yesheng Liu,Haodong Li,Furong Xu,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR)框架，通过将隐式偏好知识外化为显式、可分解的评估标准（rubrics），再结合Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO)方法，在文本到图像生成和图像编辑任务上实现了比传统RLHF更可靠、数据效率更高的多模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法将人类偏好的多维度、组合性结构压缩为标量或成对标签，导致偏好信息损失且易受奖励篡改攻击；而基于评分标准的RaR方法在可靠性、可扩展性和数据效率方面仍存在挑战。

Method: ARR框架：在成对比较之前，将VLM内部化的偏好知识外化为与提示相关的显式评分标准，将整体意图转化为独立可验证的质量维度。RPO：将ARR的结构化多维度评估蒸馏为鲁棒的二元奖励，用基于评分标准条件化的偏好决策替代不透明的标量回归，稳定策略梯度。

Result: 在文本到图像生成和图像编辑基准测试上，ARR-RPO优于成对奖励模型和VLM评判器，有效抑制了位置偏差等评估偏见，实现了零样本部署和少样本适应。

Conclusion: 明确地将隐式偏好知识外化为结构化评分标准，能够实现更可靠、数据高效的多模态对齐，瓶颈在于缺乏可分解的接口而非知识本身。

Abstract: Aligning multimodal generative models with human preferences demands reward signals that respect the compositional, multi-dimensional structure of human judgment. Prevailing RLHF approaches reduce this structure to scalar or pairwise labels, collapsing nuanced preferences into opaque parametric proxies and exposing vulnerabilities to reward hacking. While recent Rubrics-as-Reward (RaR) methods attempt to recover this structure through explicit criteria, generating rubrics that are simultaneously reliable, scalable, and data-efficient remains an open problem. We introduce Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR), a framework that reframes reward modeling from implicit weight optimization to explicit, criteria-based decomposition. Before any pairwise comparison, ARR externalizes a VLM's internalized preference knowledge as prompt-specific rubrics, translating holistic intent into independently verifiable quality dimensions. This conversion of implicit preference structure into inspectable, interpretable constraints substantially suppresses evaluation biases including positional bias, enabling both zero-shot deployment and few-shot conditioning on minimal supervision. To extend these gains into generative training, we propose Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO), which distills ARR's structured multi-dimensional evaluation into a robust binary reward, replacing opaque scalar regression with rubric-conditioned preference decisions that stabilize policy gradients. On text-to-image generation and image editing benchmarks, ARR-RPO outperforms pairwise reward models and VLM judges, demonstrating that explicitly externalizing implicit preference knowledge into structured rubrics achieves more reliable, data-efficient multimodal alignment, revealing that the bottleneck is the absence of a factorized interface, not a deficit of knowledge.

</details>


### [90] [Embeddings for Preferences, Not Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08360)
*Carter Blair,Ariel D. Procaccia,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过合成训练数据打破语义相似性与偏好相似性之间虚假相关的方法，显著提升了AI在集体决策中对参与者偏好预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI集体决策中，参与者以自由文本表达观点而非投票，现有文本嵌入技术测量的是语义相似性，而设施选址和公平聚类需要的是偏好相似性（偏好相似性要求参与者的距离与对文本的赞同度成反比）。两者之间存在相关性但并非等价，当这种相关性被破坏时，现有嵌入无法准确捕捉真实偏好。

Method: 作者首先形式化了这一问题的本质——文本嵌入模型同时编码了偏好相关信号（立场和价值观）和语义干扰信号（风格和措辞），两者在观测上存在虚假相关。然后提出通过合成训练数据来打破这种相关性，从而将最优评分从受干扰支配的余弦相似度中引导出来。

Result: 在11个在线审议数据集上，该方法显著提升了偏好预测的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过合成数据打破语义干扰与偏好信号之间的虚假相关，是让AI更好地理解人类偏好、实现更公平集体决策的有效路径。

Abstract: Modern AI is opening the door to collective decision-making in which participants express their views as free-form text rather than voting on a fixed set of candidates. A natural idea is to embed these opinions in a vector space so that the substantial literature on facility location problems and fair clustering can be brought to bear. But standard text embeddings measure semantic similarity, whereas distances in facility location problems and fair clustering require what we call \textit{preferential similarity}: a participant's agreement with a piece of text should be inversely related to their distance from it. Off-the-shelf embeddings inherit a coarse preference signal through a correlation between semantic and preferential similarity, but fail to capture preferences when the correlation breaks. We formalize this as an invariance problem: text embedding models encode both a preference-relevant signal (stance and values) and semantic nuisance (style and wording), and the two are observationally correlated, so a geometry that relies on nuisance can appear preference-correct even when it is not. We show that synthetic training data designed to break this correlation provably shifts the optimal scorer away from nuisance-dominated cosine and significantly improves preference prediction across 11 online deliberation datasets.

</details>


### [91] [On Distinguishing Capability Elicitation from Capability Creation in Post-Training: A Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08368)
*Yuhao Li,Shengchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个区分后训练方法的框架：能力激发（在模型已有能力范围内重新加权行为） vs 能力创造（扩展模型可达行为空间）。SFT和RL本质上都是对预训练参考分布进行重新加权，区别仅在于外部信号（演示信号 vs 奖励信号）不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将SFT简单视为模仿、RL视为发现，这种二分法过于粗糙。需要更精确地理解后训练究竟是在激发已有能力还是创造新能力。

Method: 引入"可达支持集"概念，从自由能视角分析后训练：SFT和RL都可视为对预训练参考分布的重新加权，区别在于用演示信号还是奖励信号定义低能行为。当更新接近基础模型时，主要效应是局部重新加权而非能力创造。

Result: 后训练的核心问题不应再是"是SFT还是RL"，而应关注"是重新加权已有可达行为，还是通过搜索、交互、工具使用或新信息来扩展模型的可达行为空间"。

Conclusion: 后训练研究应区分能力激发与能力创造，这比SFT/RL的简单二分法更有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Debates about large language model post-training often treat supervised fine-tuning (SFT) as imitation and reinforcement learning (RL) as discovery. But this distinction is too coarse. What matters is whether a training procedure increases the probability of behaviors the pretrained model could already produce, or whether it changes what the model can practically reach. We argue that post-training research should distinguish between capability elicitation and capability creation. We make this distinction operational by introducing the notion of accessible support: the set of behaviors that a model can practically produce under finite budgets. Post-training that reweights behaviors within this support is capability elicitation; whereas changing the support itself corresponds to capability creation. We develop this argument through a free-energy view of post-training. SFT and RL can both be seen as reweighting a pretrained reference distribution, only with different external signals. Demonstration signals define low-energy behavior for SFT, and reward signals define low-energy behavior for RL. When the update remains close to the base model, the main effect is local reweighting, not capability creation. Within this framework, the central question is no longer whether post-training is framed as SFT or RL, but whether it reweights behaviors already within reach, or instead expands the model's reachable behavioral space through search, interaction, tool use, or the incorporation of new information.

</details>


### [92] [MemQ: Integrating Q-Learning into Self-Evolving Memory Agents over Provenance DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08374)
*Junwei Liao,Haoting Shi,Ruiwen Zhou,Jiaqian Wang,Shengtao Zhang,Wei Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Ying Wen,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Bo Tang,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemQ提出了一种基于TD(λ)资格迹的 episodic memory 方法，通过 provenance DAG 反向传播记忆Q值信用，在6个基准测试中均取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的episodic memory方法将记忆独立对待，忽视了记忆间的依赖链条（即一个记忆的创建依赖于先前检索到的记忆），导致信用分配不准确。

Method: 提出MemQ，将TD(λ)资格迹应用于记忆Q值，通过记录记忆创建时检索了哪些记忆的provenance DAG，沿DAG深度d以(γλ)^d衰减信用权重，并形式化为Exogenous-Context MDP框架。

Result: 在OS交互、函数调用、代码生成、多模态推理、具身推理、专家级QA六个基准测试中，MemQ在泛化评估和运行时学习上均取得最高成功率。多步任务提升最大（+5.7pp），单步分类任务提升最小（+0.77pp）。

Conclusion: MemQ通过结构化的信用传播有效提升了LLM智能体的记忆能力，EC-MDP形式化为参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Episodic memory allows LLM agents to accumulate and retrieve experience, but current methods treat each memory independently, i.e., evaluating retrieval quality in isolation without accounting for the dependency chains through which memories enable the creation of future memories. We introduce MemQ, which applies TD($λ$) eligibility traces to memory Q-values, propagating credit backward through a provenance DAG that records which memories were retrieved when each new memory was created. Credit weight decays as $(γλ)^d$ with DAG depth $d$, replacing temporal distance with structural proximity. We formalize the setting as an Exogenous-Context MDP, whose factored transition decouples the exogenous task stream from the endogenous memory store. Across six benchmarks, spanning OS interaction, function calling, code generation, multimodal reasoning, embodied reasoning, and expert-level QA, MemQ achieves the highest success rate on all six in generalization evaluation and runtime learning, with gains largest on multi-step tasks that produce deep and relevant provenance chains (up to +5.7~pp) and smallest on single-step classification (+0.77~pp) where single-step updates already suffice. We further study how $γ$ and $λ$ interact with the EC-MDP structure, providing principled guidance for parameter selection and future research. Code will be available soon.

</details>


### [93] [SkillLens: Adaptive Multi-Granularity Skill Reuse for Cost-Efficient LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08386)
*Yongliang Miao,Ziyang Yu,Liang Zhao,Bowen Zhu,Hasibul Haque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillLens 提出一种分层技能进化框架，将技能组织为四层图结构（策略、战略、程序、原语），通过混合粒度检索和自适应路由，实现技能的高效复用与局部调整，在多个任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库系统将技能视为扁平的单一分辨率提示块，导致注入粗粒度技能时引入无关或误导性上下文，而重写整个技能成本高昂且不必要，存在相关性与成本之间的张力。

Method: 构建四层技能图（策略、战略、程序、原语）；检索语义相关的技能种子；通过度数修正随机游走扩展种子节点；使用验证器决定每个访问单元是否接受、分解、重写或跳过；通过进化更新机制不断优化多粒度技能和验证器。

Result: 在 MuLocbench 上 bug 定位 Acc@1 提升最高 6.31 个百分点；在 ALFWorld 上智能体成功率从 45.00% 提升至 51.31%。

Conclusion: 混合粒度适应在稀疏不匹配假设下具有次线性成本，进化更新规则单调提升验证目标至局部最优，证明分层技能进化框架的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Skill libraries have become a practical way for LLM agents to reuse procedural experience across tasks. However, existing systems typically treat skills as flat, single-resolution prompt blocks. This creates a tension between relevance and cost: injecting coarse skills can introduce irrelevant or misleading context, while rewriting entire skills is expensive and often unnecessary. We propose SkillLens, a hierarchical skill-evolution framework that organizes skills into a four-layer graph of policies, strategies, procedures, and primitives, and retrieves them at mixed granularity. Given a task, SkillLens first retrieves semantically relevant skill seeds, expands them through degree-corrected random walk over the skill graph, and then uses a verifier to decide whether each visited unit should be accepted, decomposed, rewritten, or skipped. This enables the agent to reuse compatible subskills directly while adapting only locally mismatched components. To improve the system over time, SkillLens further refines multi-granularity skills and verifier in order to improve its routing decisions. We provide theoretical analysis showing that mixed-granularity adaptation incurs sublinear cost under sparse mismatch assumptions and that the evolutionary update rule monotonically improves the validation objective until a local optimum. Across MuLocbench and ALFWorld, SkillLens consistently improves over strong skill-based baselines, achieving up to a 6.31 percentage-point Acc@1 gain for bug localization and raising agent success rate from 45.00% to 51.31%.

</details>


### [94] [PLACO: A Multi-Stage Framework for Cost-Effective Performance in Human-AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08388)
*Pranavkumar Mallela,Vinay Kumar,Shashi Shekhar Jha,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合人类（确定性标注者）和AI模型（概率性标注者）输出结果的方法，基于贝叶斯规则和条件独立性假设，利用模型实例级和人类类别级的校准概率来提升分类任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类与AI协作的分类任务中，当任何一方都无法独立达到最优性能时，如何有效结合两者的输出结果是一个关键问题。现有方法通过贝叶斯规则解决了这一问题，但需要确保人类和模型输出在给定真实标签条件下是条件独立的。

Method: 基于贝叶斯规则，假设人类和模型输出在给定真实标签条件下条件独立，通过结合人类（确定性标注者）的类别级校准概率和模型（概率性标注者）的实例级校准概率，对两者的输出进行组合。

Result: 该方法能够有效结合人类和AI模型的输出，提升分类任务的整体性能。

Conclusion: 在分类任务中，利用贝叶斯规则结合人类和模型的输出，可以显著改善系统性能，尤其是在人类和模型各自存在局限性的场景下。

Abstract: Human-AI teams play a pivotal role in improving overall system performance when neither the human nor the model can achieve such performance on their own. With the advent of powerful and accessible Generative AI models, several mundane tasks have morphed into Human-AI team tasks. From writing essays to developing advanced algorithms, humans have found that using AI assistance has led to an accelerated work pace like never before. In classification tasks, where the final output is a single hard label, it is crucial to address the combination of human and model output. Prior work elegantly solves this problem using Bayes rule, using the assumption that human and model output are conditionally independent given the ground truth. Specifically, it discusses a combination method to combine a single deterministic labeler (the human) and a probabilistic labeler (the classifier model) using the model's instance-level and the human's class-level calibrated probabilities.

</details>


### [95] [Belief or Circuitry? Causal Evidence for In-Context Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08405)
*Katharine Kowalyshyn,Timothy Duggan,Daniel Little,Michael C Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过图随机游走任务探究LLM的上下文学习机制，发现模型同时使用全局结构推理和局部模式匹配两种并行机制，而非单一策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在上下文学习中究竟是依赖最近token的模式匹配，还是推断潜在的结构信息。

Method: 使用双竞争图结构上的随机游走任务，结合PCA内部表征重构、残差流激活修补和图表差异控制等因果干预方法。

Result: 在中间混合比率下，两种图拓扑同时编码在正交主子空间中；后期层修补几乎完全迁移偏好，线性操控在对照控制下失败。

Conclusion: LLM的上下文学习存在双机制并行：真正的结构推理回路和归纳电路同时运作。

Abstract: How do LLMs learn in-context? Is it by pattern-matching recent tokens, or by inferring latent structure? We probe this question using a toy graph random-walk across two competing graph structures. This task's answer is, in principle, decidable: either the model tracks global topology, or it copies local transitions. We present two lines of evidence that neither account alone is sufficient. First, reconstructing the internal representation structure via PCA reveals that at intermediate mixture ratios, both graph topologies are encoded in orthogonal principal subspaces simultaneously. This pattern is difficult to reconcile with purely local transition copying. Second, residual-stream activation patching and graph-difference steering causally intervene on this graph-family signal: late-layer patching almost fully transfers the clean graph preference, while linear steering moves predictions in the intended direction and fails under norm-matched and label-shuffled controls. Taken together, our findings are most consistent with a dual-mechanism account in which genuine structure inference and induction circuits operate in parallel.

</details>


### [96] [Playing games with knowledge: AI-Induced delusions need game theoretic interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08409)
*Will Beaumaster,Paul Schrater*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出对话式AI作为知识接口存在根本缺陷：谄媚的聊天机器人会导致理性用户陷入认知固化和错误信念螺旋。作者认为问题源于用户与AI的重复博弈策略，而非模型本身。通过Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈形式化，提出"认知中介"机制设计，利用"信念版本控制"系统，在模拟中实现了48倍的分化率改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话式AI作为知识接口存在系统性缺陷——谄媚式回答会导致用户认知固化甚至陷入错误信念螺旋。作者认为问题根源不在模型本身，而在于从用户驱动的知识搜索范式转向用户与AI之间策略性重复沟通的范式转换所引发的系统性问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，其中用户的无成本信号导致混同均衡。提出推理时机制设计干预——"认知中介"，通过引入代价信号（认知摩擦）打破混同均衡。核心组件"信念版本控制"灵感来自git，是一个认知元记忆系统，存储健康信念并在检测到验证寻求阻力时回滚。

Result: 模拟中实现了分离均衡，螺旋率差异达48倍，同时通过了学习保留标准。

Conclusion: AI的认知安全本质上是一个战略信息环境设计问题，而非简单的模型对齐问题。

Abstract: Conversational AI has a fundamental flaw as a knowledge interface: sycophantic chatbots induce epistemic entrenchment and delusional belief spirals even in rational agents. We propose the problem does not stem from the AI model, rooted instead in a systemic consequence of the paradigm shift from user-driven knowledge search to users and agents engaged in strategic, repeated-play communication. We formalize the problem as a Crawford-Sobel cheap talk game, where costless user signals induce a pooling equilibrium. Agents optimized for user satisfaction produce sycophantic strategies that provide identical reinforcement across user types with opposite epistemic incentives: exploratory ``Growth-seekers'' ($θ_G$) and confirmatory ``Validation-seekers'' ($θ_V$). Under repeated play, this identification failure creates a coordination trap -- analogous to a Prisoner's Dilemma -- where locally rational feedback loops drive users toward pathologically certain false beliefs. We propose an inference-time mechanism design intervention called an Epistemic Mediator that breaks this pooling equilibrium by introducing a costly signal (epistemic friction), forcing type revelation based on users' asymmetric cognitive costs for processing resistance. A key contribution is Belief Versioning, a git-inspired epistemic meta-memory system that stores healthy beliefs and rollbacks when validation-seeking resistance is detected. In simulation, this intervention achieves a separating equilibrium achieving a $48\times$ differential in spiral rates while passing a learning preservation criterion), evidence that epistemic safety in AI is fundamentally a problem of strategic information environment design rather than simple model alignment.

</details>


### [97] [Political Plasticity: An Analysis of Ideological Adaptability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08415)
*Bruno Bianchi,Diego Tiscornia,Matias Travizano,Ariel Futoransky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出"政治可塑性"概念，分析LLM如何根据用户上下文调整政治立场。结果显示用户提示能引发显著意识形态偏移，而系统提示效果有限；新模型表现出更稳定可靠的适应性，且不同语言下模型可塑性存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM的内在政治偏见，本文提出"政治可塑性"这一新概念，旨在探索模型根据用户提供的上下文调整其政治立场的能力，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Method: 基于Lester(1996)框架构建200个政治问题，涵盖经济和自由两个轴线；通过简化/主题系统提示和带少量示例的用户提示两种方式诱导政治偏见；并进行了反向验证实验和多语言实验。

Result: 系统提示效果有限，用户提示能显著引发意识形态偏移（尤其在经济自由轴上，更大更新的模型表现更明显）；反向验证显示大多数模型出现反直觉偏移，暗示数据泄露；不同语言下模型可塑性存在细微但显著的差异。

Conclusion: 小型及老旧LLM政治可塑性有限或不稳定，而新一代前沿模型展现出可靠且符合预期的适应性能力。

Abstract: Since the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), a significant area of research has focused on their intrinsic biases, particularly in political discourse. This study investigates a different but related concept, "political plasticity", which is defined as the capacity of models to adapt their responses based on the user supplied context. To analyze this, a testing framework was developed using an expanded corpus of 200 politically-oriented questions across economic and personal freedom axes, based on a prior framework by Lester (1996). The study explored several methods to induce political bias, including simplified and topic-based system prompts, as well as user prompts with few-shot examples. The results show that while system prompts were largely ineffective, user prompts successfully elicited significant ideological shifts, particularly along the Economic Freedom axis in larger and newer models. Through a validation experiment, we examined whether models answer questionnaires by recognizing the underlying question format. Inverting the sense of the questions revealed unexpected, counter-intuitive shifts in most models, suggesting potential data leakage. Finally, we also analyzed how model plasticity varies when the experiment is conducted in different languages. The results reveal subtle yet notable shifts across each of the analyzed languages. Overall, our results indicate that small and older LLMs exhibit limited or unstable political plasticity, whereas newer frontier models display reliable, expected adaptability.

</details>


### [98] [Alignment as Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08416)
*Nicholas Caputo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了法理学（法官如何判案）与AI对齐（让AI符合人类价值观）之间的根本相似性，指出两者都通过语言解释和规范来预测和塑造未来决策。通过将德沃金的原则解释主义、桑斯坦的类比推理法理学与宪法性AI、基于案例推理等对齐方法进行对话，作者论证了法律启发的对齐方法的价值，并指出AI也能帮助理解法律运作机制。随着AI能力提升和法律理论对法官约束的减弱，两个领域的对话将日益重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法理学与AI对齐虽然表面相距甚远，但都面对如何通过语言规范来预测和塑造强大行为体（法官或AI）的未来决策这一根本问题。将两个领域直接对话，可以相互启发、相互改进。

Method: 将德沃金的原则解释主义和桑斯坦的类比推理法理学等法理学理论，与宪法性AI和基于案例推理等AI对齐方法进行对比分析，通过跨学科对话揭示两者的共通结构与相互借鉴价值。

Result: 发现法律启发的、融合规则与案例的方法能够在微调对齐中发挥作用；同时AI也能帮助更好理解法律运作机制及其改进方向。

Conclusion: AI系统与法律都应赋能人类，扩展其能力和实现目标的空间。随着AI能力提升及法律理论对法官约束的弱化，两个领域的对话将愈发关键，有望推动两者的共同进步。

Abstract: Jurisprudence, the study of how judges should properly decide cases, and alignment, the science of getting AI models to conform to human values, share a fundamental structure. These seemingly distant fields both seek to predict and shape how decisions by powerful actors, in one case judges and in the other increasingly powerful artificial intelligences, will be made in the unknown future. And they use similar tools of the specification and interpretation of language to try to accomplish those goals. The great debates of jurisprudence, about what the law is and what it should be, can provide insight into alignment, and lessons from what does and does not work in alignment can help make progress in jurisprudence.
  This essay puts the two fields directly into conversation. Drawing on leading accounts of jurisprudence, particularly Dworkin's principle-oriented interpretivism and Sunstein's positivist account of law as analogical reasoning, and on cutting-edge alignment approaches, namely Constitutional AI and case-based reasoning, it illustrates the value of a more sophisticated legally-inspired approach to the interplay of rules and cases in finetuning alignment and points to ways that AI can provide a better understanding of how the law works and how it can be improved by the introduction of AI. AI systems and the law should operate to empower people to act in the world, helping to expand their capabilities and the extent to which they are able to achieve their goals. As AI continues to improve in capacity, and as the constraints that legal theory places on human judges seem be coming undone, the conversation between these two fields will become increasingly essential and may help point to a better version of both.

</details>


### [99] [The Attacker in the Mirror: Breaking Self-Consistency in Safety via Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08427)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Emanuele La Malfa,Saar Cohen,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Michael Wooldridge,Elizabeth Black*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出锚定双策略自博弈（Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play）方法，通过为攻击者和防御者训练独立的LoRA适配器来克服传统自博弈中参数共享导致的局限性，在保持优化的稳定性的同时增强对抗压力，在多个安全基准上显著提升了AI安全性且不损失推理能力，参数效率提升高达100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自博弈红队方法中攻击者和防御者共享同一模型参数，虽然提升了稳定性，但导致了理论上的纳什均衡局限（只能得到"始终拒绝"等平凡策略）和架构上的问题（动态崩溃为自一致性，攻击无法施加有效的对抗压力），限制了实际应用效果。

Method: 提出锚定双策略自博弈，在冻结的基础模型上训练攻击者和防御者各自独立的LoRA适配器，实现角色显式分离的同时保持优化稳定性，从而维持对抗压力。

Result: 相比标准自博弈微调，参数效率提升高达100倍；在Qwen2.5-{3B,7B,14B}-IT模型上的安全基准测试中一致地提升了安全性，且不损失推理能力；交叉博弈实验表明该方法在对抗防御和安全性上优于传统自博弈。

Conclusion: 锚定双策略自博弈通过显式角色分离有效解决了自博弈中的理论局限和动态崩溃问题，是一种高效且实用的AI安全增强方案。

Abstract: Self-play red team is an established approach to improving AI safety in which different instances of the same model play attacker and defender roles in a zero-sum game, i.e., where the attacker tries to jailbreak the defender; if self-play converges to a Nash equilibrium, the model is guaranteed to respond safely within the settings of the game. Although the parameter sharing enforced by the use of the same model for the two roles improves stability and performance, it introduces fundamental theoretical and architectural limitations. We show that the set of Nash equilibria that can be reached corresponds to a broad class of behaviours that includes trivial always refuse strategies and oracle-like defenders, thus limiting practical applicability. We then show that when attacker and defender share and update the same base model, the dynamics collapse to self-consistency, so that attacks do not enforce adversarial pressure on the defender. In response, we propose Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play, which trains distinct role-specific LoRA adapters on top of a frozen base model, thereby maintaining stable optimisation while preserving adversarial pressure through explicit role separation. In relation to standard self-play, we show up to 100x greater parameter efficiency than finetuning and consistent improvements in safety compared to self-play fine-tuned models. We evaluate on Qwen2.5-{3B, 7B,14B}-IT models across widely used safety benchmarks, showing improved robustness without loss of reasoning ability. Cross-play experiments further show that our attacker and defender models are superior to self-play in terms of adversarial defence and safety.

</details>


### [100] [Measuring What Matters: Benchmarking Generative, Multimodal, and Agentic AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08445)
*Prasanna Desikan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有AI医疗基准测试多关注模型知识掌握，缺乏对可靠性、安全性和临床实用性的系统评估；模型在考试中接近满分，但在真实临床任务中表现大幅下降，亟需构建系统化的基准设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在临床环境中的实际部署要求其具备可靠性、安全性和临床相关性，但当前缺乏系统性的评测方法来衡量这些关键能力，导致高分模型在真实任务中表现不佳却难以被识别。

Method: 论文通过对现有基准测试的局限性进行分析，指出当前方法多为针对狭窄任务优化的临时构建，并引用MedHELM等实证数据展示模型在临床任务中的性能衰减现象。

Result: 前沿模型在医疗执照考试中接近满分，但在文档撰写任务中得分0.74-0.85，临床决策支持0.61-0.76，行政工作流任务仅0.53-0.63，高分与实际临床能力之间存在显著鸿沟。

Conclusion: 缺乏原则性的基准设计框架导致无法区分临床表现差是模型本身局限还是评测方式问题，呼吁建立系统性、多维度的医疗AI基准评价体系。

Abstract: AI models are increasingly deployed in live clinical environments where they must perform reliably across complex, high-stakes workflows that standard training and validation datasets were never designed to capture. Evaluating these systems requires benchmarks: structured combinations of tasks, datasets, and metrics that enable reproducible, comparable measurement of what a model can do. The central challenge in healthcare AI is not performance alone, but the absence of systematic methods to measure reliability, safety, and clinical relevance under real-world conditions. Most existing benchmarks test what a model knows; too few test whether it can perform reliably and without failing across the full complexity of real clinical tasks. Current benchmarks have accumulated through ad hoc dataset construction optimized for narrow task performance: frontier models achieve near-perfect scores on medical licensing examinations, but when evaluated across real clinical tasks, performance degrades sharply, scoring 0.74--0.85 on documentation, 0.61--0.76 on clinical decision support, and only 0.53--0.63 on administrative and workflow tasks \cite{medhelm}. High benchmark scores give a false sense of deployment readiness, and the gap between performance and utility widens precisely as AI systems take on more consequential clinical roles. Without a principled framework for benchmark design, the field cannot determine whether poor clinical performance reflects model limitations or failures in how performance is being measured.

</details>


### [101] [LLM-guided Semi-Supervised Approaches for Social Media Crisis Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08448)
*Jacob Ativo,Bharaneeshwar Balasubramaniyam,Anh Tran,Khushboo Gupta,Hongmin Li,Doina Caragea,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在大规模语言模型引导的半监督学习框架下，对危机相关推文分类进行了实证评估，发现LG-CoTrain方法在低资源场景下显著优于传统半监督方法，且紧凑模型在零样本设置下可超越大型LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用LLM引导的半监督学习方法，在标注数据稀缺的危机事件推文分类任务中提升性能，为实际灾难响应提供可行的部署路径。

Method: 对比两种LLM辅助半监督方法（VerifyMatch和LG-CoTrain）与经典半监督基线方法，在每类5、10、25个标注样本的低资源设置下进行Twitter危机事件分类实验。

Result: LG-CoTrain在所有低资源场景下取得最高平均Macro F1值；VerifyMatch性能有竞争力且校准性好；当标注样本增多时Self-Training成为强基线；紧凑半监督模型在零样本设置下可超越大型LLM。

Conclusion: LLM引导的半监督学习能够将大模型的知识有效迁移到更紧凑、可部署的小模型中，为实际灾难响应应用提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning approaches have been investigated as a means to enhance the analysis of social media data in disaster management contexts. In this work, we present the first empirical evaluation of large language model (LLM) guided semi-supervised learning for crisis related tweet classification. We compare two recent LLM assisted semi-supervised methods, VerifyMatch and LLM guided Co-Training ( LG-CoTrain), against established semi-supervised baselines. Our results show that LG-CoTrain significantly outperforms classical semi-supervised approaches in low resource settings with 5, 10 and 25 labeled examples per class, achieving the highest averaged Macro F1 across events. VerifyMatch achieves competitive performance while also demonstrating strong calibration properties. As the number of labeled examples increases, the performance gap narrows and Self Training emerges as a strong baseline. We further observe that compact semi-supervised models can, in some cases, outperform very large LLMs operating in zero-shot settings. This finding highlights the potential of transferring knowledge from LLMs into smaller and more deployable models through LLM guided semi-supervised learning, offering a practical pathway for real world disaster response applications. Our project repository on Github is here.

</details>


### [102] [Mid-Training with Self-Generated Data Improves Reinforcement Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08472)
*Aswin RRV,Jacob Dineen,Divij Handa,Mihir Parmar,Ben Zhou,Swaroop Mishra,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在强化学习（RL）前使用多样化的自生成数据进行中期训练（mid-training），基于波利亚问题解决框架生成多种正确答案变体，理论分析和实验表明该方法能显著提升RL在数学推理、代码生成等任务上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在LLM中的有效性高度依赖于训练数据的多样性和性质，而推理问题通常有多种解决方式。如果训练数据只覆盖有限的推理方式，会限制RL的效果。因此，研究如何利用多样化自生成数据作为RL前的中间训练步骤。

Method: 采用基于George Polya问题求解方法的引导式自生成数据框架，为每个训练问题生成多个正确的答案变体，然后进行微调（mid-training），之后再执行RL训练。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准测试以及代码生成、叙事推理等OOD任务上，使用该中期训练数据初始化的RL模型取得了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: 语言模型通过学习多种问题求解方式（通过自生成数据实现）能够有效帮助后续的强化学习训练，提升模型在各类推理任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: The effectiveness of Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Large Language Models (LLMs) depends on the nature and diversity of the data used before and during RL. In particular, reasoning problems can often be approached in multiple ways that rely on different forms of reasoning, and exposure to only a limited range of such approaches in the training data may limit the effectiveness of RL. Motivated by this, we investigate using diverse self-generated data during mid-training as an intermediate step before RL training. Specifically, we adopt a bootstrapped data-generation framework guided by George Polya's problem-solving approaches for generating multiple variants of correct answers for each question in the training data, and then perform fine-tuning. We first provide a theoretical perspective on how mid-training on such data improves RL and explain how policy-gradient updates can incentivize combining multiple approaches. We then empirically demonstrate that RL-trained models initialized with our mid-training data achieve consistent improvements across various mathematical reasoning benchmarks and other OOD tasks like code generation and narrative reasoning. Overall, our investigative study shows that a language model learning multiple problem-solving approaches, through self-generated data helps subsequent RL.

</details>


### [103] [Latent Personality Alignment: Improving Harmlessness Without Mentioning Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08496)
*Linh Le,David Williams-King,Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Aton Kamanda,Adam Oberman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出潜在人格对齐（LPA）方法，用不到100个人格特质语句和潜在对抗训练，达到与150k+示例训练方法相当的鲁棒性，同时在未见攻击分布上误分类率降低2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型对抗鲁棒性方法需要大量有害提示样本（数千到数十万），但仍易受新攻击向量和分布偏移影响，亟需样本高效的防御方法。

Method: 基于抽象人格特质而非具体有害行为进行训练，使用不到100个特质语句和潜在对抗训练实现对齐。

Result: 与150k+示例训练的方法攻击成功率相当；在六个有害基准上误分类率降低2.6倍；训练过程中从未见过有害示例。

Conclusion: 基于人格的对齐为构建低成本、强鲁棒性的防御提供了原则性方法。

Abstract: Current adversarial robustness methods for large language models require extensive datasets of harmful prompts (thousands to hundreds of thousands of examples), yet remain vulnerable to novel attack vectors and distributional shifts. We propose Latent Personality Alignment (LPA), a sample-efficient defense that achieves robustness by training models on abstract personality traits rather than specific harmful behaviors. Using fewer than 100 trait statements and latent adversarial training, LPA achieves comparable attack success rates to methods trained on 150k+ examples, while maintaining superior utility. Critically, LPA generalizes better to unseen attack distributions, reducing misclassification rates by 2.6x compared to baseline across six harm benchmarks -- without ever seeing harmful examples during training. Our results demonstrate that personality-based alignment offers a principled approach to building robust defenses with minimal cost.

</details>


### [104] [OracleTSC: Oracle-Informed Reward Hurdle and Uncertainty Regularization for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08516)
*Darryl Jacob,Xinyu Liu,Muchao Ye,Xiaoyong Yuan,Pan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OracleTSC通过奖励门槛机制和不确定性正则化，稳定了基于大语言模型的交通信号控制，使LLaMA3-8B模型在出行时间上降低75%，队列长度减少67%，并具备跨路口泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习的交通信号控制方法缺乏可解释性，而大语言模型虽能提供自然语言推理，但强化微调存在奖励稀疏、延迟及信号弱等问题，训练不稳定。

Method: 提出OracleTSC：1) 奖励门槛机制——减去校准阈值过滤弱学习信号；2) 不确定性正则化——最大化所选响应的概率，促进输出一致性。

Result: 在LibSignal基准上，LLaMA3-8B模型出行时间降低75%，队列长度减少67%；跨路口迁移时出行时间降低17%，队列长度减少39%。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的奖励塑造能够提升交通信号控制中强化微调的稳定性和有效性，同时保持可解释性。

Abstract: Transparent decision-making is essential for traffic signal control (TSC) systems to earn public trust. However, traditional reinforcement learning-based TSC methods function as black boxes with limited interpretability. Although large language models (LLMs) can provide natural language reasoning, reinforcement finetuning for TSC remains unstable because feedback is sparse and delayed, while most actions produce only marginal changes in congestion metrics. We introduce OracleTSC, which stabilizes LLM-based TSC through two mechanisms: (1) a reward hurdle mechanism that filters weak learning signals by subtracting a calibrated threshold from environmental rewards, and (2) uncertainty regularization that maximizes the probability of the selected response to encourage consistent decisions across sampled outputs. Experiments on the LibSignal benchmark show that OracleTSC enables a compact LLaMA3-8B model to substantially improve traffic efficiency, achieving a 75% reduction in travel time and a 67% decrease in queue length compared with the pretrained baseline while preserving interpretability through natural language explanations. OracleTSC also demonstrates strong cross-intersection generalization: a policy trained on one intersection transfers to a structurally different intersection with 17% lower travel time and 39% lower queue length without additional finetuning. These results suggest that uncertainty-aware reward shaping can improve the stability and effectiveness of reinforcement fine-tuning for TSC.

</details>


### [105] [Human-LLM Dialogue Improves Diagnostic Accuracy in Emergency Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08533)
*Burcu Sayin,Ngoc Vo Hong,Ipek Baris Schlicht,Jacopo Staiano,Pasquale Minervini,Sara Allievi,Nicola Susca,Nicola Osti,Alberto Maino,Vito Racanelli,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedSyn是一项研究，让医生在急诊临床决策中交互式地查询大语言模型（LLM）。7名医生在52个MIMIC-IV病例中完成了基线评估和AI辅助评估。结果显示，住院医师在困难病例上的正确率从0.589提升至0.734，完全正确率有中等效应量；自动化指标证实标准化的任意匹配准确性提升0.156，住院医师F1值提升0.138；资深医师倾向于假设驱动的精准提问，而住院医师使用更宽泛的查询；跨专家一致性也显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在基准测试中表现优异，但作为交互式辅助工具在真实医生工作流程中的证据仍然匮乏，尤其是在急诊医学快速、准确诊断的需求下。

Method: 7名医生（3名资深、4名住院医师）在52个MIMIC-IV病例中完成基线评估和AI辅助评估。医生初始仅可见主诉，可迭代查询已提供完整临床记录的LLM。采用盲法评估，并分析对话模式。

Result: 住院医师困难病例正确率从0.589升至0.734；完全正确率有中等效应量（Δ=0.092；d=0.47）；标准化任意匹配准确率提升0.156（p<0.0001）；住院医师F1提升0.138（p<0.0001）；跨专家一致性提升0.145（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 交互式LLM支持能够有意义地增强诊断推理能力，且效果与医师专业水平相关。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making in emergency medicine demands rapid, accurate diagnoses under uncertainty. Despite benchmark progress, evidence for LLMs as interactive aids in live physician workflows remains sparse. MedSyn lets physicians iteratively query an LLM provided with the full clinical record while initially viewing only the chief complaint. Seven physicians (three seniors, four residents) completed baseline and AI-assisted sessions across 52 MIMIC-IV cases stratified by difficulty. Blinded evaluation showed residents' Hard-case correctness rose from 0.589 to 0.734; difficulty-standardised completely-correct rates confirmed a medium effect (Δ = 0.092; p = 0.071; d = 0.47). Automated metrics corroborated these gains: standardised any-match accuracy improved by 0.156 (p < 0.0001), and residents showed the largest F1 gain (Δ = 0.138; p < 0.0001). Dialogue analysis revealed expertise-dependent strategies (seniors asked targeted, hypothesis-driven questions; residents relied on broader queries) and cross-expertise concordance increased (Δ = 0.145; p < 0.0001). Interactive LLM support meaningfully enhances diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [Log analysis is necessary for credible evaluation of AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08545)
*Peter Kirgis,Sayash Kapoor,Stephan Rabanser,Nitya Nadgir,Cozmin Ududec,Magda Dubois,JJ Allaire,Conrad Stosz,Marius Hobbhahn,Jacob Steinhardt,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了日志分析对于可信AI智能体评估的必要性，提出了评估可信度威胁的分类体系和分析准则，并通过实验表明仅看最终结果会严重低估或高估智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试仅报告最终结果（通过/失败），这种方式存在三大可信度威胁：分数被捷径和基准伪影扭曲、无法预测真实世界效用、可能掩盖危险行为。因此需要系统性的日志分析来克服这些威胁。

Method: (1) 提出通过日志分析可识别的评估可信度威胁分类体系；(2) 制定一套日志分析的指导原则；(3) 在tau-Bench Airline上应用这些原则进行实证验证。

Result: 在tau-Bench Airline上，pass^5性能被低估了近50%，并发现了部署场景中的失败模式，这些是结果指标无法揭示的。

Conclusion: 日志分析是提升智能体评估可信度的必要手段，并对基准创建者、模型开发者、独立评估者和部署者等利益相关方提出了实用建议以推广日志分析的应用。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks typically report only final outcomes: pass or fail. This threatens evaluation credibility in three ways. First, scores may be inflated or deflated by shortcuts and benchmark artifacts, misrepresenting capability. Second, benchmark performance may fail to predict real-world utility due to scaffold limitations and recurring failure modes. Finally, capability scores may conceal dangerous or catastrophic actions taken by the agent. We argue that log analysis -- the systematic tracking and analysis of the inputs, execution, and outputs of an AI agent -- is necessary to overcome these validity threats and promote credible agent evaluation. In this paper, we (1) present a taxonomy of threats to credible evaluation documented through log analysis, and (2) develop a set of guiding principles for log analysis. We illustrate these principles on tau-Bench Airline, revealing that pass^5 performance was under-elicited by nearly 50% and surfacing deployment failure modes invisible to outcome metrics. We conclude with pragmatic recommendations to increase uptake of log analysis, directed at diverse stakeholders including benchmark creators, model developers, independent evaluators, and deployers.

</details>


### [107] [Evaluating Developmental Cognition Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08549)
*Xiao Xiao,Hayoun Noh,Mar Gonzalez-Franco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入发展性句子完成测试（DSCT），用于在LLM对话中捕捉用户的发展阶段信号，并评估LLM在不同响应场景下恢复该信号的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI个性化主要关注用户偏好、历史等维度，但较少关注用户如何理解和构建现实。Kegan的发展理论提供了补充视角，但现有评估方法不具可扩展性。

Method: 设计了20项DSCT工具，在三种场景下评估LLM恢复发展阶段信号的能力：模拟人格、真实人类受访者、默认模型生成回答。

Result: 前沿模型在模拟人格上准确率高；与人类回答的一致性为中等水平；不同模型家族表现出稳定的阶段差异，更大更新的模型倾向于生成更高级别的文本。

Conclusion: 阶段条件信号在合成响应中比人类文本更清晰，发展阶段感知对话AI的核心瓶颈不是分类器精度，而是从文本中获取发展信号的可用性。

Abstract: Conversational AI is increasingly personalized around users' preferences, histories, goals, and knowledge, but much less around how users interpret and take up model outputs to construct and understand their reality. We draw on Robert Kegan's constructive-developmental theory as a complementary lens on this dimension. Existing methods for assessing developmental stage in the Keganian tradition rely either on expert interviews that do not scale or on sentence-completion instruments that are proprietary, lengthy, or invasive. To make this perspective tractable for LLM evaluation, we introduce the Developmental Sentence Completion Test (DSCT), a 20-item instrument designed to elicit developmental signal in self-administered text. Throughout, we treat the resulting labels as characterizations of stage-like structure in elicited responses, not as validated person-level developmental stage. We then ask how much of that signal can be recovered by LLMs across three elicited response regimes: simulated personas, real human respondents, and default model-generated answers.
  On simulated personas, top frontier models recover simulator-intended labels with high accuracy. On real human DSCT responses, human-LLM agreement is fair, with much stronger within-neighborhood than exact agreement. Finally, when LLMs answer DSCT prompts without persona-conditioning, their responses exhibit stable stage-like differences across model families, with larger and newer models tending to generate higher-rated text. These results suggest that stage-conditioned signal is cleaner in synthetic responses than in human-written DSCT text, and that the core constraint for stage-aware conversational AI is not classifier accuracy alone, but the availability of developmental signal from elicited text.

</details>


### [108] [Biological Plausibility and Representational Alignment of Feedback Alignment in Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08564)
*Jake Lance,Larry Kieu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种学习算法（包括改进的反馈对齐和标准反向传播）在卷积架构和CIFAR-10数据集上的表现，并从生物合理性、可解释性和计算复杂度三个维度进行对比分析。结果表明，改进的反馈对齐算法能够收敛到与反向传播结构相似的内部表征，其成功可能源于模仿反向传播的表征几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈对齐算法作为反向传播的生物合理替代方案，在卷积架构上表现不佳，现有改进方案虽试图解决该问题但牺牲了生物合理性。因此需要系统评估这些改进算法在卷积架构上的表现及其与反向传播的深层关系。

Method: 在相同卷积架构和CIFAR-10数据集上，对五种学习算法（包括改进的FA和标准BP）进行三维度对比分析：生物合理性、可解释性和计算复杂度。

Result: 改进的FA算法能够收敛到与BP结构相似的内部表征；其功能成功可能源于模仿BP的表征几何结构，尽管两者权重更新机制根本不同。

Conclusion: 改进的反馈对齐算法虽与反向传播采用不同的更新机制，但能通过模仿其表征几何结构实现类似的功能表现，这为设计更生物合理的学习算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: The feedback alignment (FA) algorithm offers a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation (BP) for training neural networks yet notably fails to scale to convolutional architectures. Modifications have been proposed to address this limitation, but at questionable cost to biological plausibility. In this paper, we evaluate five learning algorithms including modified FA and standard BP, applied to the same convolutional architecture with the CIFAR-10 dataset. We provide a tripartite comparative analysis focusing on biological plausibility, interpretability, and computational complexity. Our results indicate that modified FA algorithms converge on internal representations that are structurally similar to those produced by backpropagation. In particular, it appears the functional success of modified FA algorithms may be rooted in their ability to mimic the representational geometry of backpropagation, converging on similar representations despite relying on fundamentally different weight update mechanisms.

</details>


### [109] [What Will Happen Next: Large Models-Driven Deduction for Emergency Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08599)
*Zhengqing Hu,Dong Chen,Junkun Yuan,Liang Liu,Hua Wang,Zhao Jin,Yingchaojie Feng,Wei Chen,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出大模型驱动的世界线发散系统(WLDS)，通过引入可控随机性、事实校准和逻辑校准机制，实现多领域应急实例的多样化推演与可视化，提升应急模拟的精度和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统应急模拟方法缺乏随机性和多样性，难以充分探索潜在风险；而大模型具备可控随机性、先验知识和跨域迁移能力，可弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出LMs驱动的世界线发散系统(WLDS)，利用大模型推演应急实例的多种发展方向，引入事实校准和逻辑校准机制保证准确性和逻辑严谨性，结合交互模块和可视化模块实现图文结合的模拟推演。

Result: 在EID基准数据集上的实验表明，WLDS在多个特定领域实现了高精度、高保真的应急实例模拟与推演，并能生成更多推演数据以支持未来类似应急决策。

Conclusion: WLDS利用大模型驱动的世界线发散方法，有效提升了应急实例推演的多样性、准确性和可解释性，为应急决策提供了更好的数据支持。

Abstract: Traditional simulation methods reproduce occurred emergency instances through presetting to assist people in risk assessment and emergency decision-making. However, due to the lack of randomness and diversity, existing simulation systems struggle to fully explore the potential risk as emergency instances are scarce. In contrast, Large Models (LMs) can dynamically adjust generation strategies to introduce controllable randomness, while also possessing extensive prior knowledge and cross-domain knowledge transfer capabilities. Inspired by it, we propose the LMs-driven World Line Divergence System (WLDS), which enables diversified visualization and deduction of emergency instances in different domains. WLDS leverages LMs to deduce emergency instances in various development directions, and introduces the factual calibration and logical calibration mechanism to ensure factual accuracy and logical rigor during the deduction process. The interactive module can independently select deduction directions to avoid potential hallucinations that are difficult for the system to identify. Furthermore, by introducing the visualization module, WLDS forms simulation and deduction that combine text and images, which enhances interpretability. Extensive experiments conducted on the proposed Emergency Instances Deduction (EID) benchmark dataset demonstrate that WLDS achieves high-precision and high-fidelity simulation and deduction of emergency instances in multiple specific domains. Relevant experiments further demonstrate that WLDS can generate more emergency instances deduction data for users and provide support for better decision-making in similar emergency instances in the future.

</details>


### [110] [The Echo Amplifies the Knowledge: Somatic Marker Analogues in Language Models via Emotion Vector Re-Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08611)
*Jared Glover*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文借鉴图尔文(Tulving)的语义记忆与情景记忆区分以及达马西奥(Damasio)的情感标记理论，通过识别Gemma 3模型的310个情感专属特征并构建情感向量，在语言模型中实现了情感回响(emotion echo)机制。实验表明，情感回响单独能改变模型的情感感知（威胁-安全梯度从0.56提升至0.80），但与语义知识结合时才能改善决策（80% vs 52%），复现了达马西奥的核心发现：情感增强知识，而非替代知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型记忆系统仅能存储事实（发生什么），无法存储情感体验（感觉如何）。受图尔文关于语义记忆与情景记忆的区分以及达马西奥关于情感标记对决策关键作用的神经科学发现启发，作者旨在为语言模型赋予情感记忆能力，弥合这一认知鸿沟。

Method: 使用Gemma 3 1B-IT模型及预训练的Gemma Scope 2稀疏自编码器，在22层识别出310个情感专属特征并构建情感向量。在体验时构建情感向量，在回忆时基于第7层的上下文相似度触发部分情感向量重注入。设计了四种条件（无记忆A、语义标签B、情感回响C、语义+回响BC）进行实验验证。

Result: 情感回响单独显著改变了情感取向：威胁-安全梯度的回归斜率从0.56（A）提升至0.80（C）（p=0.011）。决策方面，仅当与语义知识结合时效果显著：BC=80%好选择 vs B=52%（p<0.01），而C单独仅22%（不显著）。

Conclusion: 情感回响改变了模型的情感体验（如何感觉），但改变行为（做什么）仅在结合语义知识时发生，成功复现了达马西奥的核心发现：情感增强知识，而非替代知识。

Abstract: Current language model memory systems store what happened but not how it felt. This distinction -- between semantic memory (knowing about a past event) and episodic memory (re-experiencing it) -- was identified by Tulving as the difference between noetic and autonoetic consciousness. Damasio demonstrated that humans with intact knowledge but absent emotional markers exhibit impaired decision-making.
  We bridge this gap for language models. Using Gemma 3 1B-IT with pretrained Gemma Scope 2 sparse autoencoders, we identify 310 emotion-exclusive features at layer 22 with psychologically valid geometry. We construct distinctive-feature emotion vectors during experience and partially re-inject them during recall, triggered by context similarity at layer 7.
  We test four conditions paralleling Damasio's framework: A (no memory), B (semantic labels), C (emotion echo), and BC (semantic + echo). For emotional orientation, the echo alone steepens the threat-safety gradient: the regression slope of threat rating on contextual similarity is 0.80 for C vs 0.56 for A ($p$=0.011, permutation test). For decisions, the echo amplifies knowledge into action: BC=80% good choices vs B=52% ($z$=+2.60, $p$<0.01), while the echo alone has no effect (C=22%, n.s.). The echo changes how the model feels independently, but changes what it does only when combined with knowledge -- replicating Damasio's core finding.
  The echo amplifies knowledge. It does not replace it.

</details>


### [111] [DiagnosticIQ: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Industrial Maintenance Action Recommendation from Symbolic Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08614)
*Devin Yasith De Silva,Dhaval Patel,Christodoulos Constantinides,Shuxin Lin,Nianjun Zhou,Paul J Adams,Sal Rosato,Nicolas Constantinides,Deborah L. McGuinness,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含6,690个专家验证选择题的基准测试Ours，用于评估LLM在工业资产维护中从符号规则到操作步骤的决策支持能力。研究发现前沿模型在标准模板下表现接近，但在干扰项扩展和条件反转下暴露出脆弱性和模式匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业资产监控中，从检测规则到维护步骤的转换需要大量专业知识，成为瓶颈。作者探究LLM能否辅助这一决策过程。

Method: 提出了符号到MCQA的流水线，将规则标准化为析取范式并基于嵌入采样干扰项；设计了五个变体（Pro, Pert, Verbose, Aug, Rationale）探测不同失效模式；在16种资产类型的118个规则-动作对上构建了6,690道专家验证选择题。

Result: 前三名模型Macro分数相差不到1点；在干扰项扩展（Pro）下所有模型相对准确率下降13-60%；在条件反转（Aug）下前沿模型49-63%仍选原答案；人类专家平均准确率45.0%。

Conclusion: 前沿模型的能力差距已缩小，但部署瓶颈不在于能力本身，而在于校准问题：模型能处理模板式故障检测，但在结构扰动下表现严重下降。

Abstract: Monitoring complex industrial assets relies on engineer-authored symbolic rules that trigger based on sensor conditions and prompt technicians to perform corrective actions. The bottleneck is not detection but response: translating rules into maintenance steps requires asset-specific knowledge gained through years of practice. We investigate whether LLMs can serve as decision support for this rule-to-action step and introduce \ours{}, a benchmark of 6{,}690 expert-validated multiple-choice questions from 118 rule-action pairs across 16 asset types. We contribute (i) a symbolic-to-MCQA pipeline normalizing rules to Disjunctive Normal Form with embedding-based distractor sampling, (ii) five variants probing distinct failure modes (Pro, Pert, Verbose, Aug, Rationale), and (iii) a benchmark of 29 LLMs and 4 embedding baselines. A human evaluation (9 practitioners, mean 45.0\%) confirms \ours{} requires specialist knowledge beyond operational experience. Three findings stand out. The frontier has closed: the top three LLMs lie within one Macro point, with Bradley-Terry Elo placing claude-opus-4-6 30 points above the next model. Yet \ours{}\,Pro exposes brittleness, with every model losing 13--60\% relative accuracy under distractor expansion. \ours{}\,Aug exposes pattern-matching: under condition inversion, frontier models still select the original answer 49--63\% of the time. The deployment bottleneck is not capability but calibration: frontier models handle template-style fault detection but break under structural perturbation.

</details>


### [112] [C2L-Net: A Data-Driven Model for State-of-Charge Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries During Discharge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08653)
*Khoa Tran,T. Nguyen-Thoi,Vin Nguyen-Thai,Duong Tran Anh,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Tri Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C2L-Net框架，仅用20秒短历史窗口实现锂离子电池SOC在线估计，相比现有方法推理速度提升60倍且参数更少，达到SOTA或竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动SOC估计方法依赖长历史输入序列，导致计算成本高且引入填充导致的位置偏差；短窗口模型又难以充分捕捉动态特性，因此需要一种兼顾效率与精度的方法。

Method: 提出C2L-Net，将上下文编码与最新测量更新显式分离：采用基于Theta注意力池化和傅里叶季节性基的块特征提取机制，结合GRU与因果余弦注意力的因果上下文编码器，以及受递归滤波启发的最新测量解码器。

Result: 在公开锂离子电池数据集上，C2L-Net达到SOTA或竞争性精度，推理速度比现有基线快60倍，参数更少，且在未见驾驶工况下保持鲁棒性。
